## Summary

AIDCF-007 Master Control Framework and Control Catalogue v1.0

15 domains \| 90 control families \| 360 normative controls \| Draft for controlled review

| Control domains | 15 |  | GOV | Governance, Accountability and Assurance Direction | 6 | 24 | CG-13; CG-14 |
| Control families | 90 |  | CAP | AI Capability, Service Outcome and Demand Management | 6 | 24 | CG-01; CG-02 |
| Normative controls | 360 |  | DEP | Dependency, Concentration and External Reliance Management | 6 | 24 | CG-03; CG-12 |
| Controls per family | 4 |  | PHY | Physical Site, Building and Security Infrastructure | 6 | 24 | CG-02; CG-04 |
| Artifact ID | AIDCF-MCF-001 |  | POW | Electrical Power Infrastructure and Energy Continuity | 6 | 24 | CG-05 |
|  |  |  | THM | Cooling, Thermal and Fluid Infrastructure | 6 | 24 | CG-06 |

|  |  |  | SUP | Supply Chain, OEM and Ecosystem Assurance | 6 | 24 | CG-03; CG-13 |

| MCF-GOV-001 | GOV | Governance, Accountability and Assurance Direction | GOV-01 | Governance architecture | Governance Charter | Ensure governance charter is defined, implemented, maintained and demonstrably supports the applicable AI Capability Instance and Service Outcomes. | The accountable owner shall establish and maintain governance charter, define its scope, dependencies, operating-state applicability, performance or acceptance criteria, roles, evidence, exceptions and reassessment triggers. The implementation shall be reconciled to the approved architecture and evaluated for credible failure, common-mode exposure and recovery implications. | 1) Scope and accountable owner are approved; 2) requirements and acceptance criteria are measurable; 3) implementation is traceable to affected capabilities and dependencies; 4) applicable failure and degraded-state conditions are analysed; 5) evidence is current and conflicts or UNKNOWN are visible; 6) material changes trigger review. | charters; approved policy; role matrix; minutes; independent review records | Document and architecture review; stakeholder interview; sample trace | Governance / directive | Design; Build; Commission; Operate; Change | NORMAL and approved design state | Apply where the function, asset, service, provider or dependency is material to a Capability Instance or Service Outcome. | APS-044; APS-049; APS-050; APS-053 | CG-13; CG-14 | Organisation; Role; Responsibility; Decision | unclear authority; delayed decision; unmanaged exception | CapabilityInstance - SUPPORTS/DEPENDS_ON - InfrastructureEntity; Safeguard - PROTECTS - Function; EvidenceObject - SUPPORTS/DISPUTES - Assertion | A2 Design | Use authorised, competent and least-intrusive methods. Do not perform destructive or hazardous testing without explicit authority and engineering safety governance. | If material evidence, ownership, state, path or dependency is unresolved, record UNKNOWN and apply the relevant assurance ceiling or critical-gate consequence. | Material architecture, capacity, supplier, configuration, operating-state, incident, failure, evidence-expiry or service-outcome change affecting governance charter. | AIDCF-003; AIDCF-004; AIDCF-005; AIDCF-006; future AIDCF-008 and AIDCF-009 | Draft for controlled review | 1.0 |

Do not perform destructive or hazardous testing without explicit authority and engineering safety governance. | If material evidence, ownership, state, path or dependency is unresolved, record UNKNOWN and apply the relevant assurance ceiling or critical-gate consequence. | Material architecture, capacity, supplier, configuration, operating-state, incident, failure, evidence-expiry or service-outcome change affecting performance outcomes. | AIDCF-003; AIDCF-004; AIDCF-005; AIDCF-006; future AIDCF-008 and AIDCF-009 | Draft for controlled review | 1.0 |

Do not perform destructive or hazardous testing without explicit authority and engineering safety governance. | If material evidence, ownership, state, path or dependency is unresolved, record UNKNOWN and apply the relevant assurance ceiling or critical-gate consequence. | Material architecture, capacity, supplier, configuration, operating-state, incident, failure, evidence-expiry or service-outcome change affecting design-to-operation traceability. | AIDCF-003; AIDCF-004; AIDCF-005; AIDCF-006; future AIDCF-008 and AIDCF-009 | Draft for controlled review | 1.0 |

Do not perform destructive or hazardous testing without explicit authority and engineering safety governance. | If material evidence, ownership, state, path or dependency is unresolved, record UNKNOWN and apply the relevant assurance ceiling or critical-gate consequence. | Material architecture, capacity, supplier, configuration, operating-state, incident, failure, evidence-expiry or service-outcome change affecting cloud and service dependency. | AIDCF-003; AIDCF-004; AIDCF-005; AIDCF-006; future AIDCF-008 and AIDCF-009 | Draft for controlled review | 1.0 |

Do not perform destructive or hazardous testing without explicit authority and engineering safety governance. | If material evidence, ownership, state, path or dependency is unresolved, record UNKNOWN and apply the relevant assurance ceiling or critical-gate consequence. | Material architecture, capacity, supplier, configuration, operating-state, incident, failure, evidence-expiry or service-outcome change affecting access and logistics. | AIDCF-003; AIDCF-004; AIDCF-005; AIDCF-006; future AIDCF-008 and AIDCF-009 | Draft for controlled review | 1.0 |

Do not perform destructive or hazardous testing without explicit authority and engineering safety governance. | If material evidence, ownership, state, path or dependency is unresolved, record UNKNOWN and apply the relevant assurance ceiling or critical-gate consequence. | Material architecture, capacity, supplier, configuration, operating-state, incident, failure, evidence-expiry or service-outcome change affecting contention management. | AIDCF-003; AIDCF-004; AIDCF-005; AIDCF-006; future AIDCF-008 and AIDCF-009 | Draft for controlled review | 1.0 |

Do not perform destructive or hazardous testing without explicit authority and engineering safety governance. | If material evidence, ownership, state, path or dependency is unresolved, record UNKNOWN and apply the relevant assurance ceiling or critical-gate consequence. | Material architecture, capacity, supplier, configuration, operating-state, incident, failure, evidence-expiry or service-outcome change affecting model and artifact tier. | AIDCF-003; AIDCF-004; AIDCF-005; AIDCF-006; future AIDCF-008 and AIDCF-009 | Draft for controlled review | 1.0 |

Do not perform destructive or hazardous testing without explicit authority and engineering safety governance. | If material evidence, ownership, state, path or dependency is unresolved, record UNKNOWN and apply the relevant assurance ceiling or critical-gate consequence. | Material architecture, capacity, supplier, configuration, operating-state, incident, failure, evidence-expiry or service-outcome change affecting 24x7 coverage. | AIDCF-003; AIDCF-004; AIDCF-005; AIDCF-006; future AIDCF-008 and AIDCF-009 | Draft for controlled review | 1.0 |

Do not perform destructive or hazardous testing without explicit authority and engineering safety governance. | If material evidence, ownership, state, path or dependency is unresolved, record UNKNOWN and apply the relevant assurance ceiling or critical-gate consequence. | Material architecture, capacity, supplier, configuration, operating-state, incident, failure, evidence-expiry or service-outcome change affecting release provenance. | AIDCF-003; AIDCF-004; AIDCF-005; AIDCF-006; future AIDCF-008 and AIDCF-009 | Draft for controlled review | 1.0 |

## README

AIDCF-007 Workbook Usage Notes

| Topic | Guidance |
|---|---|
| Purpose | Companion machine-readable catalogue and assessment template for AIDCF-007. |
| Constitutional rule | Controls do not create assurance by existence. Apply AIDCF-006 evidence, UNKNOWN, gate and bounded-decision rules. |
| Applicability | Start from a Capability Instance, Service Outcomes, architecture, dependencies, states and decision need. |
| N/A | Use only with approved rationale. Missing evidence is UNKNOWN, not N/A. |
| Scores | Do not calculate a favourable aggregate that overrides critical gates or UNKNOWN. |
| Evidence | AIDCF-008 will formalise evidence taxonomy and grades. |
| Assessment | AIDCF-009 will formalise procedures and decision workflow. |
| Safety | Testing must be authorised, competent and least intrusive. |
| Status | Draft for controlled review; multidisciplinary review remains pending. |
